Conclusion: MultiMoQ通过多接入路径协同和灵活的分块调度机制，有效解决了带宽受限条件下高码率沉浸式视频流的鲁棒传输问题，为大规模并发用户提供更平滑的播放体验，优于传统DASH和标准MoQ方案。

Abstract: Live immersive video streaming, particularly 360-degree video, is increasingly adopted in applications such as virtual events, sports broadcasting, and remote education. Existing approaches struggle to support high-bitrate immersive streaming for large numbers of concurrent users, with coarse-grained delivery limiting responsiveness and insufficient support for coordinating concurrent tile streams. Media over QUIC (MoQ) has recently emerged as a promising solution for large-scale media delivery, yet it lacks robustness under bandwidth-constrained conditions, often resulting in playback stalls. To address these challenges, we present MultiMoQ, a multi-access tile streaming framework built on MoQ that redesigns its delivery mechanism for robust high-bitrate streaming across multiple access paths while supporting flexible tile scheduling and seamless access switching. We implement a fully functional prototype of MultiMoQ and evaluate it in network emulation under heterogeneous real-world network conditions, comparing against Dynamic Adaptive Streaming over HTTP (DASH) and standard MoQ. Results show that MultiMoQ increases goodput for enhancement tiles and base video and reduces enhancement-tile tail end-to-end latency relative to DASH, while preserving audio continuity and avoiding the persistent stalls of standard MoQ. These transport gains translate into smoother viewport playback, and the ablation results further confirm the contribution of multi-access control to playback continuity.

</details>


### [84] [From Passive Mirrors to Active Agents: Holonic Digital Twins for Physical AI over Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06227)
*Christo Kurisummoottil Thomas,Omar Hashash,Walid Saad*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出HDT-Net框架，通过分层全息数字双胞胎和主动推理机制，解决AI在物理系统中难以应对不确定性和未见场景的问题，实现物理智能的实时协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI工具（如深度学习和生成式AI）在嵌入遵循物理规律的物理系统时经常失败，无法在不确定性下维护可靠的世界模型进行长时域规划，也难以泛化到未见场景。当前无线网络架构仅优化吞吐量、延迟和可靠性，无法支持需要共享时空上下文的实时物理AI协调。

Method: 提出全息数字双胞胎网络（HDT-Net）框架。每个HDT为跨越物理代理和网络边缘的分层结构，在局部自主推理的同时与邻近HDT协作。利用因果马尔可夫毯跨越感知、通信和控制，确定需协调的代理并支持多域干预的反事实推理。通过主动推理最小化预期自由能，统一感知、行动和学习；利用范畴论保持跨异构代理的语义结构；采用整合信息理论量化集体智能涌现。

Result: HDT-Net能够实现物理AI的实时推理，通过分层全息代理主动推理环境而非被动镜像物理资产，实现自主局部推理和全局协作智能。该框架支持基于认知价值的信息传输、跨异构系统的语义一致性保持，并能量化集体智能何时超越独立操作。

Conclusion: HDT-Net框架通过主动推理、范畴论和整合信息理论的结合，为无线网络支持物理智能编排提供了新范式，实现了从传统性能优化向实时物理AI协调的转变。

Abstract: Despite advances in artificial intelligence (AI) across multiple sectors, today's AI tools, including deep learning and generative AI, still fail when embedded into physical systems, such as robots and vehicles operating under real-world physical laws. This stems from their inability to maintain reliable world models for long-horizon planning under uncertainty and generalize to unseen scenarios. In this context, wireless networks, through pervasive sensing and communication, can orchestrate physical intelligence. However, current architectures optimize throughput, latency, and reliability and cannot support real-time physical AI coordination, requiring agents to maintain shared spatiotemporal context. To address these challenges, a network of holonic digital twins (HDT-Nets) framework is proposed to deliver real-time physical AI inference through holonic agents that actively reason about their environment rather than passively mirror physical assets. Each HDT is realized as a hierarchical structure spanning the physical agent and network edge, reasoning autonomously at the local level while cooperating with neighboring HDTs to form collectively intelligent units. In HDT-Net, causal Markov blankets spanning sensing, communication, and control determine which agents must coordinate and enable counterfactual reasoning over multi-domain interventions. Active inference within these boundaries unifies perception, action, and learning by minimizing expected free energy while deciding which beliefs to transmit based on their cognitive value to the receiver. Category theory ensures that transmitted beliefs preserve semantic structure across heterogeneous agents with incompatible representations. Finally, integrated information theory quantifies when collective intelligence exceeds independent operation and how network intelligence evolves through coordinated learning and information exchange.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [Hierarchical Server Architecture for Agentic Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05332)
*Vanessa Sochat,Daniel Milroy*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个层次化、动态的资源发现架构，通过秘书代理实现跨云、边缘和HPC系统的自动化资源发现与调度，在模拟实验中实现了87.71%的协商准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理科学（Agentic science）正在改变计算工作流程，工作负载需要跨机构的专用硬件资源。若要在调度时评估工作负载需求与环境匹配，自动化资源发现是必不可少的关键步骤。

Method: 提出层次化、动态的架构和软件系统，利用秘书代理（secretary agents）实现跨云、边缘和HPC系统的并发异步协商、选择和调度。代理程序探测并发现7个类别中的51个真实和模拟资源提供者。

Result: 通过19,973次协商和6,952次选择模拟实验，展示了87.71%的协商准确率，选择成本与传统策略相当，证明了决策的高可靠性。

Conclusion: 该架构展现了代理程序、发现工具和基础设施之间协调对于代理科学的重要性，设计具有可扩展性，目前支持Genesis Mission任务。

Abstract: Agentic science is transforming the landscape of computational work, extending to scientific pipelines and workload managers. The workloads require specialized hardware within and across institutions. If assessing workload needs against environments is required for scheduling, automated discovery of resources is an essential step. In this paper, we present a hierarchical, dynamic architecture and software to discover resources across diverse cloud, edge, and HPC systems. The design enables concurrent, asynchronous negotiation, selection, and dispatch of requests for work using secretary agents. The agents probe and discover 51 real and simulated providers across 7 categories. We perform 19,973 negotiation and 6,952 selection simulations to assess reliability of decisions, demonstrating high (87.71\%) negotiation accuracy and selection costs comparable to more traditional strategies. Designed for extensibility and currently supporting the Genesis Mission, this architecture exemplifies the importance of careful coordination between agents, discovery tools, and infrastructure for agentic science.

</details>


### [86] [Application Failures and Machine Computational Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05408)
*Carlo Graziani,Bethany Lusch,O. E. Bronson Messer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对艾级科学计算机正常运行时间效率的评估框架，考虑到应用故障率不可忽视的情况，并通过Frontier超算一年的生产运行数据进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前顶级科学计算平台和大型AI训练设施面临应用故障率显著的问题。科学计算平台的应用异质性要求故障率和平均故障间隔应以使用量（如节点小时）而非时间来衡量，这促使作者重新审视和更新现有框架。

Method: 作者考虑了故障、检查点和重启成本导致的使用损失，更新了Daly（2006）的框架，使用户能够指定最优检查点使用间隔以最小化损失，并推导出机器计算效率指标。该框架使用Frontier超算一年的生产运行数据进行方法验证。

Result: 建立了以使用量为基础的故障率指标体系，推导出机器计算效率公式，能够量化预期可用于科学计算的资源分配比例，并通过Frontier超算实际数据验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为艾级科学计算平台提供了更准确的正常运行时间效率评估方法，能够帮助用户优化检查点策略，最大化科学计算资源的可用性。

Abstract: We present a framework for evaluating uptime efficiency of Exascale-class scientific computers when application failure rates are appreciable. This is the situation that confronts current leadership-class scientific computing platforms and large AI training installations. What distinguishes scientific computing platforms is the heterogeneity of their applications. We argue that this diversity requires that failure rates and mean intervals between failures should be specified in terms of \emph{usage} (e.g. node-hours) rather than time, as is currently customary. We consider the usage loss terms due to failures, to checkpointing, and to restart costs, and update the framework of Daly (2006) allowing users to specify optimal checkpointing usage intervals that minimize such losses. We derive the machine computational efficiency, which specifies the expected fractional resource allocation that is available for scientific computation. We illustrate the methodology using one year of production runtime data from the \emph{Frontier} supercomputer at Oak Ridge National Laboratory.

</details>


### [87] [Serverless platform driven CPU loadbalancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05633)
*Abdul Rehman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个serverless平台驱动的CPU负载均衡框架，通过自定义Linux调度器让控制平面直接参与CPU调度决策，在降低15%系统能耗的同时仅增加5%调用开销，并在高负载场景下将请求延迟降低50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有serverless平台虽然拥有全局的函数调用和资源利用视图，但CPU调度决策主要依赖操作系统调度器，平台无法充分利用全局信息进行优化。这种限制阻碍了平台在能耗优化和性能提升方面的潜力。

Method: 基于SchedExt(SCX)构建自定义Linux调度器，引入可配置的调度域和共享接口。控制平面根据函数的历史到达间隔时间将其分配到不同调度域。在每个域内采用单队列负载均衡策略结合虚拟时间优先级策略，改善任务放置并减少忙轮询任务的干扰。

Result: 八域配置达到最佳权衡：系统能耗降低约15%，调用开销仅增加5%。在高负载场景下，请求延迟相比默认Linux CFS调度器降低高达50%。

Conclusion: 将CPU调度决策暴露给serverless控制平面可以在保持调度灵活性的同时，同时提升能效和工作负载性能，证明了平台驱动调度的有效性。

Abstract: Serverless platforms maintain a global view of function invocations and resource utilization, yet existing systems largely restrict CPU scheduling decisions to the operating system scheduler. This paper presents a serverless platform-driven CPU load balancing framework that enables the control plane to directly influence CPU scheduling through a custom Linux scheduler built on SchedExt(SCX). The proposed scheduler introduces configurable scheduling domains and a shared interface that allows the control plane to assign functions to domains based on their historical inter-arrival times. Within each domain, a single-queue load-balancing strategy combined with a virtual-time prioritization policy improves task placement while reducing interference from busy-polling tasks. Results show that an eight-domain configuration achieves the best trade-off, reducing system energy consumption by approximately 15% while increasing invocation cost by only 5%. Under heavily loaded workloads, the proposed scheduler also reduces request latency by up to 50% compared to the default Linux Completely Fair Scheduler (CFS). These results demonstrate that exposing CPU scheduling decisions to the serverless control plane can improve both energy efficiency and workload performance while preserving scheduling flexibility.

</details>


### [88] [RepoOMP: Repository-Aware Hotspot OpenMP Parallelization via Dependency-Aware Context Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05855)
*Yongjie Qian,Ke Gao,Zhibin Zhang,Shaohui Peng,Ling Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了RepoOMP框架，通过多粒度属性性能图(MAP)和结构化转换上下文(STC)实现规则与LLM代理的混合并行化，在真实仓库热点上取得了显著的加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OpenMP并行化工具存在局限性：基于规则的工具在合法性无法局部证明时会欠并行化；基于代理的方法在检索遗漏关键依赖或包含无关代码时表现不稳定。热点并行化的安全性和优化收益往往依赖非局部证据，使得成熟仓库中的并行化变得困难。

Method: 提出RepoOMP混合框架：1)构建多粒度属性性能图(MAP)恢复并行化相关证据；2)通过路由机制在确定性规则和LLLM代理之间分配热点；3)构造结构化转换上下文(STC)，向模型暴露依赖事实而不淹没无关代码。该框架在生成前先恢复并行化相关证据。

Result: 在NPB、BOTS、FFmpeg、NCNN和GROMACS的951个热点上评估：372个热点被接受（含330个真实仓库热点）；NPB平均加速8.23×，BOTS平均加速8.96×；9个详细真实内核跨骨干网平均加速5.25×，相比非结构化Claude Code基线加速提升18-28%，代理侧token成本降低47-68%；330个真实热点中位数加速2.25×。

Conclusion: RepoOMP为仓库环境下的热点并行化提供了证据引导的工作流，有效结合了规则方法的确定性和LLM代理的灵活性，在保持正确性的同时实现了显著的性能提升和成本降低。

Abstract: OpenMP parallelization of hotspots in mature repositories remains difficult because loop safety and optimization payoff often depend on non-local evidence. Rule-based tools under-parallelize when legality is not locally provable, while agent-based approaches become unstable when retrieval misses decisive dependencies or includes irrelevant code. We present RepoOMP, a hybrid framework that recovers parallelization-relevant evidence before generation. RepoOMP builds a Multi-granularity Attributes Performance graph (MAP), routes hotspots between deterministic rules and an LLM agent, and constructs a Structured Transformation Context (STC) that exposes dependency facts without flooding the model with unrelated repository text. We evaluate RepoOMP on 951 profiled hotspots from NPB, BOTS, FFmpeg, NCNN, and GROMACS. Under compilation, workload-specific checks, and positive speedup, 372 hotspots are accepted, including 330 real-world repository hotspots. RepoOMP achieves average speedups of $8.23\times$ on NPB and $8.96\times$ on BOTS. For the nine detailed real-world kernels used in matched-backbone and robustness analyses, RepoOMP reaches a cross-backbone mean of $5.25\times$, improves speedup by 18--28\%, and reduces agent-side token cost by 47--68\% relative to the unstructured Claude Code baseline. Across 330 accepted real-world hotspots, median speedup is $2.25\times$. Overall, RepoOMP provides an evidence-guided workflow for hotspot parallelization in repository settings. The open-source repository is available at https://github.com/Qlalq/RepoOMP_Simplified.

</details>


### [89] [Operating Multi-Node Full Fine-Tuning on NVIDIA B300: A Field Report on Telemetry-Based Triage, Negative Results, and Operational Hardening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05944)
*Seon Ho Kim,Ui Jeong Jeon,Su Hyeon Kim,Min Tae Hwang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文报告了在16块NVIDIA B300 GPU上全参数微调32.76B参数模型（Qwen3-32B）的实践经验，提供了四项工程产物：功耗诊断表、负面实验结果（揭示常见优化误区）、性能基准数据和故障解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在新型NVIDIA B300加速器上缺乏大规模模型全参数微调的公开实践经验，需要为后续研究者提供经过校准的硬件数据和可靠的工程实践参考，以避免常见的部署陷阱和故障。

Method: 使用16块NVIDIA B300 GPU（双节点）和FSDP/ZeRO-3技术对32.76B参数的Qwen3-32B模型进行全参数微调，采用对照实验和性能剖析方法，对功耗、数据加载策略、扩展性和故障模式进行系统性测量和分析。

Result: （1）建立了B300功耗诊断表，可通过板级功率区分计算、通信、数据饥饿、检查点/死锁和空闲状态；（2）负向结果显示NFS读取与本地缓存性能相当（约53k tok/s），通过性折叠源于NFS/CPU竞争而非存储限制；（3）4/8/16 GPU强扩展接近线性；（4）发现epoch结束时NCCL死锁问题并开发2.7秒预运行不变量检查门控机制。

Conclusion: 对于数据依赖的数据并行任务，应优先监控功耗而非利用率指标，并在启动前验证不变量约束——仅通过冒烟测试并不能保证全程安全运行。该研究提供了可转移的运维经验而非算法创新。

Abstract: We report operational experience full-fine-tuning a 32.76B-parameter dense model (Qwen3-32B) on 16 x NVIDIA B300 (two nodes, FSDP / ZeRO-3) -- among the first published field accounts on this accelerator. We claim no new algorithm. The individual mechanisms we use are established practice; our contribution is the integrated field experience and a set of calibrated measurements on new hardware. Concretely we offer four practitioner artifacts. (1) A B300-calibrated power-draw triage table that distinguishes compute / communication / data-starvation / checkpoint-or-deadlock / idle by board wattage (utilization% reads 100% during an NCCL hang). (2) A set of honest negative results that dispel common optimization folklore at this scale: a controlled A/B in which per-step NFS reading matches a pretokenized local cache (~53k tok/s) because the corpus fits in page cache and the job is compute-bound; and a reconstruction of an earlier "throughput collapse" as NFS/CPU contention rather than a storage-medium limit. (3) Calibrated 4/8/16-GPU strong-scaling and GPU-hour numbers on B300 (near-linear, as expected in this regime; we report absolute values as reference data). (4) A worked failure case -- an epoch-end NCCL deadlock from per-rank token-packing imbalance -- together with a 2.7-second pre-run invariant gate and an external watcher that turn multi-hour silent failures into instant rejections. This deadlock and its remedy correspond to PyTorch's documented Join / equalize-to-minimum practice; we position our instantiation against that prior art and report the GPU-hours the failure cost and the gate saves. The transferable takeaway is operational, not algorithmic: for data-dependent data-parallel jobs, watch power rather than utilization, and verify invariants before launch -- a passing smoke test is not evidence of a safe full run.

</details>


### [90] [TensorCast: The Missing Tensor Management Layer in Large Language Model Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06007)
*Yuhan Zhou,Yuchu Luo,Hao Nie,Wangrunze Lv,Yu Zhou,Yibo Zhu,Daxin Jiang,Chenren Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出TensorCast，一个分布式张量管理层，通过TaaS抽象解耦张量状态管理与计算逻辑，在保持与专用系统相当性能的同时，实现了跨组件优化，在高并发场景下TTFT提升高达93.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基础设施针对不同张量管理任务（如模型权重加载、KV缓存管理、检查点同步等）构建了专用机制，但这些机制与执行引擎、网络或存储后端深度耦合，形成了相互隔离的孤岛，阻碍了张量管理策略在演进LLM工作负载中的重用和组合。

Method: 识别张量生命周期管理作为LLM基础设施中缺失的抽象层，提出Tensor-as-a-Service（TaaS）概念，设计与实现TensorCast分布式张量管理系统。TensorCast提供一等张量抽象、可编程生命周期原语，以及一个将张量管理策略与执行机制分离的运行时，使开发者能够通过API编写张量管理程序并透明地利用分布式执行和数据移动能力。

Result: 将TensorCast集成到vLLM和SGLang中，在模型权重物化、权重同步、KV缓存管理和可编程请求路由等多种张量生命周期工作负载上进行了评估。结果表明，TensorCast在实现与专用张量管理系统相当性能的同时，能够支持新的跨组件优化策略，在高并发多轮智能体工作负载下，中位TTFT提升高达93.2%。

Conclusion: 张量生命周期管理是LLM基础设施中被忽视的关键抽象层。通过解耦张量状态管理与计算逻辑，TensorCast成功消除了现有系统的孤立问题，实现了张量管理策略的统一与重用，在保证性能竞争力的同时开启了新的跨组件优化可能性。

Abstract: Modern LLM infrastructure increasingly manages tensors not only as computation data, but also as persistent states shared across distributed components. Existing systems optimize individual tensor management tasks, such as model weight loading, KV cache management, and checkpoint synchronization, by deeply integrating task-specific mechanisms with execution engines, networks, or storage backends. However, this specialization creates isolated silos that hinder the reuse and composition of tensor management strategies across evolving LLM workloads. In this paper, we identify tensor lifecycle management as a missing abstraction layer in LLM infrastructure and propose Tensor-as-a-Service (TaaS), which decouples tensor state management from computation logic. We design and build TensorCast, a distributed tensor management layer that provides first-class tensor abstractions, programmable lifecycle primitives, and a runtime that separates tensor management policies from execution mechanisms. This enables developers to write tensor management programs using TensorCast APIs while transparently leveraging distributed execution and data movement. We integrate TensorCast with vLLM and SGLang and evaluate it across diverse tensor lifecycle workloads, including model weight materialization, weight synchronization, KV cache management, and programmable request routing. Our results show that TensorCast achieves competitive performance with specialized tensor management systems while enabling new cross-component optimization policies. A programmable policy implemented with TensorCast improves median TTFT by up to 93.2% under highly concurrent multi-turn agent workloads.

</details>


### [91] [Routing LLM Inference to the Cleanest Grid in Real Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06188)
*Aleks Bernhard,Arif Baran Yardimci*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究通过实时验证和年度历史数据回放，证明了基于边际运行排放率（MOER）信号的碳感知推理路由可将GPU推理运营碳排放降低最高50.9%，实现了在不更改硬件或重训练模型的前提下有效降低碳排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理的电力需求快速增长，其边际碳排放强度在不同电网区域和一天中的不同时段差异巨大（相差超过一个数量级），这使得请求放置成为一个极具吸引力的减排杠杆：无需重新训练模型，也无需更改硬件设备。

Method: 在多区域GPU测试平台上进行了碳感知推理路由的实时验证，采用边际运行排放率（MOER）信号驱动。研究具有三个独特属性：1）使用实际生产压力路由器作为盲基线，而非均匀放置；2）通过测量并发曲线和NVIDIA DCGM遥测数据归因每个请求的能耗，而非使用标称TDP；3）对每个请求进行碳结算，基于历史MOER而非仅依赖预测值。研究还回放了一年的每小时MOER数据进行建模分析。

Result: 实时验证证明了MOER信号驱动跨区域推理的可行性，未观察到调度失败。年度建模结果显示，碳感知放置相比轮询策略可减少50.9%的GPU运营排放（95%置信区间48.5-53.3%）。在会话固定之前，每小时最低MOER路由策略贡献约22.4个百分点的减排，约占总减排量的40%，超越了静态年均MOER策略。研究建议在比较区域时应按绝对MOER值排序，而非区域内标准化的百分位信号索引。

Conclusion: 碳感知推理路由在实践中可行且有效，可严格、可逆地叠加于生产路由器之上。由于回放基于历史MOER而非预测值，所报告的减排量为该配置下的上限；预测误差会降低实际实现的节省量。研究结果针对特定机群和历史年份，并非普适性估算。

Abstract: Large-language-model inference is a fast-growing electricity load whose marginal carbon intensity varies by more than an order of magnitude across grid regions and across the day, making request placement an attractive lever: no retraining, no hardware change. We report a live validation of carbon-aware inference routing on multi-region GPU testbeds driven by marginal operating emissions rate (MOER) signals, with three properties uncommon in prior work: a blind baseline that is an actual production pressure-based router rather than uniform placement; per-request energy attributed from GPU telemetry (NVIDIA DCGM) via measured concurrency curves rather than nameplate TDP; and carbon settlement of every request against historical MOER, not only the forecast that drove the decision. The central live result is feasibility: a MOER signal steered inference across regions with no observed dispatch failures, as a strict and reversible overlay on the production router. To size the effect, we replay a year of hourly MOER across a grid-diverse CONUS fleet. In the primary modeled configuration, carbon-aware placement reduces modeled GPU-attributable operational emissions by 50.9% versus round-robin (95% block-bootstrap CI 48.5-53.3%). Because the replay dispatches against historical MOER rather than a forecast, this is an upper bound under that configuration; forecast error would reduce operationally realized savings. Before session pinning, hourly lowest-MOER routing contributes about 22.4 percentage points, roughly 40% of the 54.0% placement reduction, beyond a static annual-mean-MOER policy. These are modeled results for one fleet and historical year, not a universal estimate. We also record a practical observation: when comparing regions, rank by absolute MOER rather than the percentile signal-index, which is normalized within each region and answers a temporal, not a spatial, question.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [Agentic Nesting: A New Methodology for Existing Enterprise Application Integration and Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05159)
*Xi Wang,Kun Li,Xianyao Ling,Gang Yin,Liang Zhang,Jiang Wu,Wenbo Lei,Jun Xu,Annie Wang,Fu Zhang,Weizhe Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Nesting框架，通过"应用即代理"范式将遗留企业应用封装为自主AI代理，采用分层嵌套结构和自然语言对话实现异构系统低成本、智能化的集成与编排。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业依赖多个异构业务系统导致严重的数据孤岛和流程碎片化问题，传统集成方法（ESB、API网关、RPA）存在架构耦合高、运维成本上升和智能能力有限等固有缺陷，难以有效利用和编排现有应用。

Method: 提出Agentic Nesting多代理协作框架，核心包括：(1)将现有企业应用封装为自主AI代理并组织成分层监管拓扑结构；(2)从每个遗留应用提取数字代理代理以支持自然语言交互和自主操作；(3)通过中央编排器进行任务分解和动态调度；(4)暴露统一的对话接口实现跨应用查询和流程编排。

Result: 提出了"Application-as-Agent"集成范式和"Conversation-as-Integration"交互理念，构建了能够支持异构系统协调和大规模数据应用场景的多代理协作框架。

Conclusion: 该方法在异构系统协调和大规模数据应用等场景中展现出良好的泛化潜力，为企业应用集成提供了更智能化、低耦合的解决方案。

Abstract: Enterprise operations extensively rely on multiple heterogeneous business systems and information applications, which also result in severe data silos and process fragmentation. Enterprises have invested considerable financial and material resources in building these applications, however, effectively leveraging and orchestrating them remains a formidable challenge. Conventional approaches to enterprise application integration, encompassing middleware architectures such as Enterprise Service Bus (ESB), API gateway infrastructures, and Robotic Process Automation (RPA), suffer from inherent limitations like high architectural coupling, escalating operation and maintenance costs, and limited intelligence capabilities. This paper proposes Agentic Nesting, a multi-agent collaboration framework in which existing enterprise applications are encapsulated as autonomous AI agents within a hierarchically nested structure. Rather than flat interconnection, agents are organized into layered stewardship topologies that mirror the compositional complexity of enterprise ecosystems. The framework extracts a digital agent proxy from each legacy application to enable natural-language interaction and autonomous manipulation, coordinates multiple agents through a central orchestrator for task decomposition and dynamic dispatching, and exposes a unified conversational interface for cross-application querying and process orchestration. The main contributions of this paper are the proposition of the "Application-as-Agent" integration paradigm and the "Conversation-as-Integration" interaction philosophy, together with an exploration of the generalization potential of this methodology in scenarios encompassing heterogeneous system coordination, and large-scale data applications.

</details>


### [93] [The Ignition Index: Measuring Global Workspace Dynamics in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05160)
*Saman Rahbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Ignition Index (I)，一个将全局工作空间理论(GWT)的"全或无"点火预测在Transformer语言模型中量化的标量指标。研究发现在11个模型中，前馈Transformer比状态空间模型(SSM)表现出更高的点火值(89%)，循环架构在递归维度上展现类工作空间转换，并观察到Pythia-410M在训练步骤256出现相变。该指标首次建立了GWT动态预测与机制可解释性之间的定量桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局工作空间理论(GWT)预测意识处理具有"全或无"的点火特性，但在Transformer语言模型中缺乏可操作的量化指标来验证这一预测。现有研究缺少能够定量测量GWT动态预测并与机制可解释性建立联系的方法。

Method: 提出Ignition Index (I)指标，通过对每层线性探针准确率拟合四参数sigmoid函数作为输入信号强度的函数，提取陡峭度参数beta-hat。高值表示突然的点火式转换，低值表示渐进式积累。使用shuffled-label控制实验验证指标的测量选择性，在11个模型、五种架构家族上进行实验分析。

Result: (1) 前馈Transformer的聚合beta-hat比SSM高89%(p < 1e-13)，Mamba展现近线性特征；(2) Huginn-3.5B沿迭代轴的点火比深度轴高2.12倍；(3) Pythia-410M在训练步骤256显示PELT检测的相变(+67%)，先于感应头形成；(4) 模型规模和信号强度与点火的关联未被证实。指标对真实语言结构的测量选择性达到9.6倍(p < 0.001)。

Conclusion: Ignition Index提供了首个经过验证的定量方法来连接GWT动态预测与机制可解释性，具有9.6倍的测量选择性和架构级区分能力，为理解Transformer语言模型中的全局广播机制提供了新工具。研究表明不同架构表现出不同的点火特征，循环架构沿递归维度展现类工作空间转换，且点火可能在训练过程中早于特定机制形成。

Abstract: We introduce the Ignition Index (I), a validated scalar metric that operationalizes Global Workspace Theory's (GWT) all-or-none ignition prediction in transformer language models. The metric fits a four-parameter sigmoid to per-layer linear probe accuracy as a function of input signal strength, extracting steepness parameter beta-hat: high values indicate abrupt, ignition-like transitions; low values indicate graded build-up. Across 11 models spanning five architecture families, shuffled-label controls demonstrate 9.6-fold selectivity for genuine linguistic structure over spurious probe capacity (p < 0.001, Mann-Whitney U-test). We find: (1) Feedforward transformers exceed SSMs by 89% in aggregate beta-hat (p < 1e-13, Cohen's d = 0.52), with Mamba exhibiting near-linear profiles consistent with absent global broadcast. (2) Huginn-3.5B exhibits 2.12-fold higher ignition along its iteration axis than its depth axis, demonstrating that recurrent architectures manifest workspace-like transitions along the recurrence dimension. (3) Pythia-410M shows a PELT-detected phase transition at training step 256 (+67%), preceding induction-head formation. (4) Hypotheses linking ignition to model scale and signal strength were not confirmed, suggesting transformer architectures may saturate available ignition mechanisms. The Ignition Index provides the first validated quantitative bridge between GWT's dynamical predictions and mechanistic interpretability, with 9.6-fold measurement selectivity and architecture-level discriminability not previously characterized in the scaling literature. Code: https://github.com/saman-rahbar/ignition-index

</details>


### [94] [Woodpecker Distillation: Weak Models Diagnose Reasoning Bugs in Strong Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05168)
*Dayu Wang,Jiaye Yang,Weikang Li,Jiahui Liang,Yang Li,Deguo Xia,Jizhou Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Woodpecker Distillation框架，通过对比弱模型的局部干预来修正强模型的推理错误，从而提升大语言模型的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然具备解决推理任务的能力，但常因中间步骤的局部推理错误而失败，且直接微调无法有效内化修正信号。

Method: 提出Woodpecker Distillation弱到强训练框架，在相同前缀下对比成功与失败的弱模型补丁，构建修正性教师分布，并将其蒸馏到强模型中。

Result: 在数学推理基准测试中，该方法持续提升强模型性能，并优于直接模仿基线。

Conclusion: Woodpecker Distillation证明了通过对比局部干预提取的修正信号可有效提升强模型推理能力，为弱到强学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models often fail on reasoning tasks despite possessing the capability to solve them. We argue that many such failures arise from localized reasoning bugs in intermediate steps rather than from global incompetence. We show that these bugs are frequently repairable: inserting a short patch generated by a weak probe model after the same strong-model reasoning prefix can redirect the trajectory toward a correct solution.
  However, this corrective effect is not reliably internalized by directly fine-tuning on weak patches or repaired trajectories, suggesting that the useful signal lies not in the intervention text itself, but in how it reshapes the model's future reasoning distribution. We therefore propose Woodpecker Distillation, a weak-to-strong training framework that learns from contrastive local interventions. Our method contrasts successful and unsuccessful weak-model patches at the same prefix, constructs a corrective teacher distribution from their induced future token predictions, and distills this signal into the strong model.
  Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that Woodpecker Distillation consistently improves strong-model performance and outperforms direct imitation baselines.

</details>


### [95] [From Continuous Predictors to Clinical Thresholds: Early Evidence on Performance Trade-offs of Guideline-Based Categorisation for Ischaemic Stroke Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05203)
*Esra Zihni,Katryna Cisek,Hamzah Ziadeh,Hendrik Knoche,Robert Mikulik,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨用临床指南分类编码替代连续变量构建卒中预测模型，在多中心数据上发现两者性能基本相当，核心特征重要性保持一致，为临床可解释模型设计提供了可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在急性缺血性卒中90天预后预测中表现优异，但由于模型解释与临床推理不一致，临床采用受限。一项临床医生用户研究呼吁采用与临床指南一致的截断值，因此本文探讨是否可以用临床导向的分类编码替代连续预测变量而不损失模型性能。

Method: 在按治疗队列分层的多中心欧洲注册数据上，对比标准梯度提升模型与完全分类的梯度提升模型，后者使用卒中指南对齐、治疗特异性的阈值对连续变量进行离散化处理。

Result: 在三个治疗队列中，完全分类模型在其中两个队列上与连续变量模型性能统计上无显著差异，一个队列预测准确率显著下降；全局特征重要性排序在各队列中保持一致，表明基于指南的分类保留了预后因素的核心层级结构。

Conclusion: 基于指南的分类是卒中预后模型的可行设计选择，能够在保持核心预后因素重要性的同时，提高模型的可解释性与临床采纳度。

Abstract: Machine learning models achieve strong predictive accuracy for 90-day outcome prediction in acute ischaemic stroke, yet clinical adoption is limited by the misalignment of model explanations with clinicians' reasoning. Motivated by a clinician user study calling for clinical guideline-aligned cut-offs, we ask whether continuous predictors can be replaced by clinically informed categorical encodings without sacrificing performance. On a multi-centre European registry stratified into three treatment cohorts, we compare standard and fully categorised gradient-boosted models, the latter using stroke guideline-aligned, treatment-specific thresholds. The fully categorised models are statistically indistinguishable from their continuous counterparts in two of the treatment cohorts, with a significant drop in predictive accuracy in one cohort. Global feature importance rankings remain consistent, suggesting that discretising continuous predictors into guideline-based categories preserves the core hierarchy of prognostic factors across all treatment groups. Guideline-based categorisation is thus a viable design choice for stroke-outcome models.

</details>


### [96] [DoctorAgents: an agentic framework to iteratively refine AutoML pipeline for small clinical temporal data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05375)
*Ruilin Wang,Bo-Hong Wang,Elizabeth Kourbatski,Jun Bai,Hegang Chen,Ziyang Song,Gilles Boire,Marie Hudson,Yue Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DoctorAgents框架，通过LLM智能体自主构建和优化临床机器学习流程，在小规模临床数据上性能优于传统AutoML基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床机器学习部署面临数据稀缺、异构性和时序复杂性约束，现有AutoML系统依赖暴力搜索、缺乏显式推理和记忆能力，开发有效流程耗时且易出错。

Method: 提出DoctorAgents智能体框架，采用专用LLM智能体负责生成、验证和优化，通过自然语言反馈的文本梯度下降进行定向更新，实现从暴力搜索到推理驱动的策略转变。

Result: 在多样化临床任务实验中，DoctorAgents一致性地超越既有AutoML基线，同时生成更具可解释性的任务特定表示。

Conclusion: 将AutoML从穷举搜索重构为推理驱动优化，为小规模临床数据提供了高效、可解释的智能体解决方案。

Abstract: Clinical machine learning (ML) has the potential to support high-stakes medical decision-making, but reliable deployment is often constrained by scarce, heterogeneous, and temporal complexity. Developing effective ML pipelines for such data remains time-consuming and error-prone, while existing automated machine learning (AutoML) systems only partially address this challenge because they largely rely on brute-force search over predefined spaces and lack explicit reasoning and memory. We therefore reformulate AutoML for small clinical data from exhaustive search to reasoning-driven refinement. We propose DoctorAgents, an agentic AI framework that autonomously constructs and optimizes end-to-end ML pipelines through specialized large language model (LLM) agents for generation, validation, and refinement. DoctorAgents backpropagates natural-language feedback through textual gradient descent to perform targeted updates without exhaustive search. Experiments across diverse clinical tasks show that DoctorAgents consistently outperforms established AutoML baselines while producing more interpretable task-specific representations.

</details>


### [97] [SkillTrace: Multi-Trace Provenance Auditing for LLM-Agent Skill Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05204)
*Jialuo Chen,Minghe Wang,Lingqi Jiang,Jianan Ma,Xinhao Deng,Xiaohu Du,Ruixiao Lin,Yunhao Feng,Linkang Du,Jingyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SKILLTRACE框架，用于检测LLM-agent生态系统中技能的多模态复用问题，在基准测试中达到AUROC 0.938。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码克隆检测方法仅针对单模态源代码或整体包相似性，而LLM-agent技能包含元数据、自然语言指令、代码、工具等多模态元素，复用证据分布在文本、实现片段和操作结构中，现有方法会遗漏仅保留技能部分内容的复用情况。

Method: SKILLTRACE提取三条溯源轨迹：表达轨迹、实现轨迹和操作轨迹。操作轨迹表示为技能操作图(SOG)，捕捉激活、过程和资源流结构。LLLM仅辅助操作轨迹提取（摄入时一次性使用），审计时通过缓存轨迹进行确定性比较，并针对同功能严格负样本校准每条轨迹。

Result: 在SKILLTRACE-BENCH基准测试中（包含820个转换复用正样本、100个市场锚点和751个负对照），SKILLTRACE达到AUROC 0.938和F1 0.898。在36,446个技能的实地审计中，轨迹归因证据发现了超越仓库级基线的可操作复用审查队列。

Conclusion: SKILLTRACE通过多轨迹溯源审计有效解决了LLM-agent技能复用检测问题，证明了多轨迹方法能够发现单模态检测器遗漏的复用情况，为技能市场的审计提供了实用工具。

Abstract: LLM-agent ecosystems are rapidly growing around reusable skills: mixed-modality packages of metadata, natural-language instructions, code, tools, references, and operational workflows. As skills become marketplace artifacts, auditing their reuse is no longer the same problem as ordinary code clone detection. Existing detectors target single-modality source code or whole-package similarity, yet skill reuse evidence is distributed across authored text, implementation fragments, and operational structure. As a result, they can miss reuse that preserves only one part of a skill. We present SKILLTRACE, a multi-trace provenance auditing framework for LLM-agent skill reuse. SKILLTRACE extracts three provenance traces: Expression, Implementation, and Operational. It represents the Operational Trace as a Skill Operational Graph (SOG) that captures activation, procedure, and resource-flow structure. An LLM assists only the Operational-trace extraction, once at ingestion; at audit time SKILLTRACE compares cached traces deterministically, calibrates each trace against same-function strict negatives, and reports which trace supports a reuse decision. On SKILLTRACE-BENCH, with 820 transformed reuse positives over 100 marketplace anchors and 751 negative controls, SKILLTRACE achieves AUROC 0.938 and F1 0.898. A 36,446-skill wild audit further shows that trace-attributed evidence surfaces actionable reuse review queues beyond repository-level baselines.

</details>


### [98] [Adaptive Arena-based Contestable Argumentative Network-of-Experts for Open-Ended Care Plan Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05391)
*Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Hoang-Loc Cao,Phuc Ho,Phuc Truong Loc Nguyen,René Richard,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CANOE是一个多智能体神经符号框架，通过五个模块解决医疗护理计划协调中的透明性和安全性问题，并在临床正确性、安全性和可解释性方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗护理计划协调需要综合跨多学科的临床、功能和心理社会信息，而现有的单体大语言模型（LLM）管道无法以透明或安全的方式执行任务，存在可解释性差和缺乏人类干预机制的问题。

Method: 提出CANOE框架，包含五个核心模块：复杂度评估、自适应团队招募、基于Arena的定量双极论证框架（A-QBAF）的角色论证计算、人在环中的争议机制、以及护理计划合成。角色专业化智能体为候选干预措施生成支持和攻击论证，通过arena冲突解决机制处理分歧，并计算可接受性分数。医疗规划者可接受、拒绝、编辑或添加论证，框架将确定性地重新计算最终计划。

Result: 在Discharge Me!和MedicalRAG数据集上，使用ROUGE-L、AlignScore、MEDCON F1、FKGL和LLM-as-a-judge等指标评估，医学微调模型在临床正确性和安全性方面表现最强，同时CANOE的论证结构提供了可信的解释和人类争议机制。

Conclusion: CANOE框架成功解决了医疗护理计划协调中的透明性和安全性问题，通过神经符号方法和论证结构实现了可解释的决策过程和人类干预能力，为医疗决策支持系统提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Care plan coordination demands synthesizing heterogeneous clinical, functional, and psychosocial information across multiple professional disciplines, where monolithic LLM pipelines cannot perform in a transparent or safe manner. We present CANOE (Contestable Argumentative Network-of-Experts), a multi-agent neuro-symbolic framework that addresses these limitations through five modules: complexity assessment, adaptive team recruitment, role-based argumentative computation via an Arena-based Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Framework (A-QBAF), human-in-the-loop contestation, and care-plan synthesis. Role-specialized agents generate supporting and attacking arguments for candidate interventions; conflicts are resolved through arena-based clash resolution before acceptability scores propagate across the argumentation graph. Care planners may accept, reject, edit, or add arguments, and the framework will deterministically recompute the final plan. Evaluation on Discharge Me! and MedicalRAG using ROUGE-L, AlignScore, MEDCON F1, FKGL, and LLM-as-a-judge shows that medically fine-tuned models achieve the strongest clinical correctness and safety, while CANOE's argumentative structure provides faithful explanation and human contestability.

</details>


### [99] [Abstract Event Causal Rules: Induction and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05205)
*Ziwei Zheng,Peiqiong Chen,Bang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出抽象事件因果规则（AECR），通过多智能体系统从具体因果对中提取可泛化的抽象因果逻辑，并构建知识库以提升低频和未见过事件组合的因果推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实例级因果对在低频长尾和未见过的事件组合上存在严重的泛化不足问题，限制了事件中心智能分析系统在风险预警、决策支持和叙事理解等任务中的表现。

Method: 提出抽象事件因果规则（AECR）范式，设计多智能体具体到抽象因果归纳系统（CACI）结合相似性约束聚类从噪声数据中提取AECR；构建两个完整的AECR知识库；提出抽象规则引导的因果注意力编码器（AR-GCAE），通过规则引导注意力层和门控表示融合将检索到的AECR注入因果图事件预测（CGEP）任务。

Result: 在CGEP基准任务上的定量实验表明，应用AECR显著增强了事件因果推理的泛化能力，为事件预测带来一致的性能提升，在罕见和未见过事件样本上获得最显著的增益。

Conclusion: 抽象级别的因果知识能够有效解决实例级因果知识的泛化瓶颈，为事件因果推理提供了新的研究范式，在实际应用中具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: Event-centric intelligent analytical systems heavily depend on explicit causal event knowledge for risk early warning, decision-making support and narrative comprehension. Nevertheless, existing instance-level causal pairs suffer severe generalization deficits on low-frequency long-tail and unseen event combinations. To address this limitation, this work proposes Abstract Event Causal Rule (AECR), a novel relation-level causal abstraction paradigm that transforms concrete cause-effect pairs into generalized abstract causal logic while retaining their intrinsic causal relationships. We design a multi-agent Concrete-to-Abstract Causal Induction (CACI) system coupled with similarity-constrained clustering to distill trustworthy AECRs from noisy raw causal data, based on which two complete AECR knowledge bases are built. To validate the practical utility of abstract causal knowledge, we propose an Abstract Rule-Guided Causal Attention Encoder (AR-GCAE), which injects the retrieved AECRs into the causality Graph Event Prediction (CGEP) benchmark task via rule-guided attention layers and gated representation fusion. Quantitative experimental results reveal that applying AECRs substantially strengthens the generalization capacity of event causal reasoning and brings consistent performance improvements to event prediction, with the most prominent gains observed on rare and unseen event samples.

</details>


### [100] [Otter: A Time-Aware, History-Conditioned Human Chess AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05206)
*Tarun Kumar S*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Otter是一个15.3M参数的人机国际象棋AI，通过将下棋建模为时间感知的序列过程而非孤立位置，在更少参数和训练数据的情况下超越了先前的最先进模型Maia 2，达到55.23%的top-1和90.95%的top-5预测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人机国际象棋模型将每个棋局位置视为孤立状态进行处理，忽略了下棋作为一个具有时间依赖性的序列活动的本质特征，包括开局偏好、位置演变倾向以及时间压力下的行为模式。这种位置单一的处理方式限制了模型预测人类棋手走法的能力。

Method: 提出Otter模型，将国际象棋建模为时间感知的序列过程。该方法结合两个条件信号：（1）移动历史编码器，基于最近20步移动进行条件预测，捕捉开局偏好、位置演变和局内行为倾向；（2）时间控制模块，根据时钟压力调节预测。模型在单块T4 GPU上使用来自1.17亿场Lichess快棋赛的61亿个位置进行30天训练。

Result: Otter实现了55.23%的top-1和90.95%的top-5走法预测准确率，以更少的参数（15.3M）和更少的训练数据超越了先前的最先进人机国际象棋模型Maia 2。在11个Elo等级分段（<1100到>=2000）中，准确率在1900-1999分段达到峰值57.38%。

Conclusion: 将国际象棋建模为时间感知的序列活动比仅基于位置的方法能产生更准确的人类走法预测，且可以使用更小的模型实现。这证明了序列建模和时间感知条件机制在人机AI建模中的有效性。研究已开源代码、训练模型和完整训练日志。

Abstract: Otter is a 15.3M-parameter human chess AI that predicts human move selection by modeling play as a time-aware, sequential process rather than treating each position in isolation. It combines two conditioning signals: (1) a move history encoder that conditions predictions on the last 20 moves, capturing opening preferences, positional drift, and intra-game behavioral tendencies; and (2) a time control module that modulates predictions based on clock pressure. Otter is trained on 6.1 billion positions from 117 million Lichess rapid games over 30 days on a single T4 GPU.
  Otter achieves 55.23% top-1 and 90.95% top-5 move-prediction accuracy, surpassing the prior state-of-the-art human chess model, Maia 2, with far fewer parameters and less training data. Across 11 Elo brackets (<1100 to >=2000), accuracy peaks at 57.38% in the 1900-1999 bracket. These results show that modeling chess as a time-aware, sequential activity yields more human-accurate move prediction than position-only approaches, using a smaller model. Code, trained models, and complete training logs are publicly released.

</details>


### [101] [SearchAuditor: Auditing and Attributing Failures in Long-Horizon Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05212)
*Zhixiang Liang,Yifei Liu,Yidan Huang,Haozhe Zhao,Beichen Huang,Jiaqi Wang,Nan Duan,Qiong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SearchAuditBench基准和SearchAuditor框架，用于评估和提升LLM在诊断深层搜索代理失败方面的能力，显著提高了错误定位、归因和修复的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深层搜索代理在处理复杂问题时需要长时间的网络交互，这一过程既复杂又脆弱：小的推理错误可能在长且嘈杂的轨迹中传播，最终导致流畅但错误的答案。诊断此类失败非常困难，需要人工检查极长的执行轨迹，这超出了人类的能力范围。

Method: 提出了SearchAuditBench基准，包含1,243条失败轨迹（平均73.1条消息和65.1K token），涵盖5个深层搜索基准上的8个开源模型，每条轨迹都有专家标注的关键错误步骤、搜索特定的根本原因和参考修复方案。同时提出了SearchAuditor框架，通过多视角审计和基于证据的判定来定位、归因和修复搜索代理失败。

Result: 即使是最强的基线方法配合GPT-5.5等前沿模型，端到端通过率也仅为26.6%。相比之下，SearchAuditor在不同前沿模型上均优于所有基线，达到32.3%的端到端通过率，且通过修复失败的运行使代理能够更好地从错误中恢复。

Conclusion: SearchAuditBench为评估LLM审计深度搜索代理失败提供了系统化基准，SearchAuditor框架能够有效减轻人类负担，显著提升搜索代理的失败诊断和修复能力。

Abstract: Deep search agents tackle challenging questions through long-horizon web interactions, a process that is both complex and fragile: small reasoning errors may propagate through long, noisy trajectories into fluent but incorrect answers. Diagnosing such failures is difficult, requiring the manual inspection of extremely long execution traces, which could be beyond human capacity. We therefore introduce SearchAuditBench, a benchmark that evaluates whether LLM auditors can localize, attribute, and repair these failures, thereby reducing the human burden. SearchAuditBench comprises 1,243 failed trajectories, averaging 73.1 messages and 65.1K tokens, collected from eight open-weight models on five deep-search benchmarks, each expert-annotated with the critical error step, a search-specific root cause, and a reference repair with grading rubrics. We further propose SearchAuditor, a multi-perspective auditing framework that effectively localizes, attributes, and repairs search-agent failures through evidence-grounded adjudication. Experimental results show that even the strongest baseline, when powered by a frontier model like GPT-5.5, attains only a 26.6% end-to-end pass rate. In contrast, our SearchAuditor consistently outperforms all baselines across different frontier models, achieving an end-to-end pass rate of 32.3%, and resuming failed runs with its repairs enables agents to better recover from errors.

</details>


### [102] [PD-GS: Phoneme-Driven 3DGS for Audio-Driven Talking Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05218)
*Ao Fu,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PD-GS方法，通过融合音素标记解决3D高斯泼溅语音头像中的"漏嘴"问题，实现更准确的唇部运动合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D高斯泼溅语音头像存在唇部运动过度平滑和违反发音约束（如双唇闭合）的问题，产生"漏嘴"伪影。核心难点在于：从连续声学嵌入推断离散发音事件的回归目标会偏向平均嘴型配置；现代自监督语音编码器虽丰富但缺乏显式帧对齐的语言学目标来可靠区分闭合级事件。

Method: 提出音素驱动高斯泼溅(PD-GS)：1) 利用ASR和强制对齐流水线获取时间对齐的音素标记；2) 设计语言学融合模块(LFM)，通过学习门控自适应融合连续音频上下文与离散音素嵌入，在保持平滑音频驱动动态的同时增强发音关键段的音素引导；3) 仅使用单目视频进行训练，采用图像重建和唇部关键点监督。

Result: 在HDTF数据集上，PD-GS在对比基线中取得最佳唇部几何精度(LMD 2.66)，定性上减少了挑战性音素序列中的闭合违规，生成更具语言学准确性的神经头像。

Conclusion: 通过引入离散音素监督与连续音频特征的融合机制，有效解决了3DGS语音头像中唇部 articulation 不准确的核心问题，为高质量语音驱动头像合成提供了新思路。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables fast, photorealistic talking-head rendering, yet accurate lip articulation remains elusive: mouth motion is often over-smoothed and may violate hard articulatory constraints such as bilabial closures, producing the notorious ``leaky mouth'' artifact. A key difficulty is that brief, discrete articulatory events are inferred from a continuous acoustic embedding under a regression objective, which biases predictions toward averaged mouth configurations. While modern self-supervised speech encoders provide rich prosodic and phonetic cues, they do not provide an explicit, frame-aligned linguistic target that reliably disambiguates closure-level events. We propose \textbf{Phoneme-Driven Gaussian Splatting (PD-GS)}, which augments a 3DGS talker with time-aligned phoneme tokens obtained from an automatic ASR and forced-alignment pipeline. Our core component, the \textbf{Linguistic Fusion Module (LFM)}, adaptively fuses continuous audio context with discrete phoneme embeddings through a learned gate, allowing the model to preserve smooth audio-driven dynamics while strengthening phoneme guidance on articulation-critical segments. PD-GS is trained purely from monocular video using image reconstruction and lip landmark supervision. On HDTF, PD-GS achieves the best lip geometry among the compared baselines (LMD 2.66) and qualitatively reduces closure violations in challenging phoneme sequences, yielding more linguistically faithful neural avatars.

</details>


### [103] [From Siloed Algorithms to Compliance-First Agentic Platforms: A Multi-Layered Architecture for Hospital AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06112)
*Manideep Dhar,Ritwik Singh,Sharat Chandra Kumar Manikonda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种面向医院的合规优先代理式AI架构，通过多智能体工作流编排、集中化政策合规管理和隐私保护数据架构，解决当前医疗AI部署碎片化和扩展失败的问题，并基于合成数据验证了其在降低任务周转时间和手动文档工作方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医院AI部署多为孤立的点解决方案，受困于部门壁垒，导致重复劳动、风险隐蔽和企业价值未实现。尽管医疗AI市场增长迅速，但约70-80%的试点项目因治理缺失、数据碎片化和缺乏集成蓝图而无法规模化，亟需一种系统性的架构解决方案。

Method: 提出一种医院特定的合规优先Agentic AI架构，在现有医院AI平台模型基础上扩展三层：(i) 智能体编排层，用于跨临床、运营和财务领域的多智能体工作流；(ii) 合规与策略层，将HIPAA、GDPR、欧盟AI法案、印度DISHA和DPDP法案及ISO/IEC标准集中为策略即代码；(iii) 隐私保护数据架构，集成联邦学习、差分隐私和安全飞地至真实HIMS流程。使用合成但结构真实的医院数据集和开源原型进行端到端编排验证。

Result: 基于原型系统成功实现了分诊风险预测、工作流优化和合规日志的端到端编排，在模拟环境中显著降低了任务周转时间和手动文档工作量，同时保持了策略防护的数据访问控制，验证了架构的可行性和实用性。

Conclusion: 该架构为医院领导者提供了从临时工具向治理良好、全球合规、以ROI为核心的AI平台转型的务实蓝图，支持本地、混合云和云原生部署，有望解决医疗AI规模化应用的核心挑战。

Abstract: Hospitals are rapidly adopting artificial intelligence for triage, imaging, scheduling etc., yet most deployments remain isolated point solutions locked inside departmental silos, resulting in duplicated effort, hidden risks, and unrealized enterprise value. Despite explosive growth of AI in healthcare market and accelerating investment, an estimated 70-80% of healthcare AI pilots fail to scale, largely due to governance gaps, fragmented data, and missing integration blueprints. This research proposes a hospital-specific, compliance-first, Agentic AI architecture with multiple interoperable layers, extending existing hospital AI platform models with: (i) an Agent Orchestration Layer for multi-agent workflows across clinical, operational, and financial domains, (ii) a Compliance and Policy Layer that centralizes policy-as-code for HIPAA, GDPR, the EU AI Act, DISHA Act, India's DPDP Act, and ISO/IEC security and safety standards, and (iii) a Privacy-Preserving Data Fabric that plugs federated learning, differential privacy, and secure enclaves into real-world Hospital Information Management System (HIMS) flows. Using a synthetic but structurally realistic hospital dataset and an open, ready-to-deploy prototype implementation, this study demonstrates the end-to-end orchestration of triage risk prediction, workflow optimization, and compliance logging, achieving substantial simulated reductions in task turnaround times and manual documentation effort while maintaining policy-guarded data access. The resulting architecture offers hospital leaders a pragmatic blueprint to move from ad hoc tools to a governed, globally compliant, ROI-focused AI platform that can be tailored to on-premise, hybrid and cloud-native deployments.

</details>


### [104] [When Privileged Guidance Misaligns: State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05219)
*Junzhuo Liu,Weiwei Li,Jun Ling,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SMRC-SD方法，通过状态匹配路由和情境化自蒸馏解决多轮智能体特权蒸馏中的状态-参考不匹配问题，在ALFWorld和WebShop上显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特权策略蒸馏在多轮智能体训练中存在状态-参考不匹配问题：当学生的动作偏离参考轨迹或以不同顺序完成子目标时，执行状态会发生变化，导致参考轨迹无法为实际到达的状态提供可靠指导，盲目应用特权蒸馏会产生不准确的监督信号。

Method: 提出状态匹配路由与情境化自蒸馏（SMRC-SD）方法：1）状态匹配路由：在每轮验证学生当前执行状态是否与参考轨迹上的支持状态匹配，仅在匹配状态处应用蒸馏，过滤掉缺乏局部兼容指导的轮次；2）情境化自蒸馏：为每个匹配状态从成功轨迹构建状态条件的教师上下文，使监督信号基于实际到达的状态。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上，SMRC-SD一致优于无条件成功全路径蒸馏方法。使用Qwen3-1.7B模型，ALFWorld任务成功率从0.746提升至0.865，WebShop从0.574提升至0.693。消融实验验证了选择局部支持轮次和构建状态兼容教师上下文都对性能提升有贡献。

Conclusion: 通过显式确定特权轨迹何时以及如何指导在线策略学生，SMRC-SD有效解决了特权蒸馏中的状态-参考不匹配问题，为多轮智能体训练提供了更可靠的监督信号，代码已开源。

Abstract: Privileged on-policy distillation provides dense supervision for multi-turn agents by allowing a synchronized teacher to re-score the student's response at every turn with access to training-only references, such as successful trajectories. In interactive environments, however, the student's preceding actions continually change the execution state. As the student takes different actions or completes subgoals in a different order, its rollout may reach states not covered by the reference, making the reference an unreliable source of guidance for the state actually reached. Applying privileged distillation indiscriminately therefore creates state--reference mismatch. This mismatch motivates a central objective: providing privileged reference guidance that remains compatible with the student's current execution state. We introduce State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation (SMRC-SD), which explicitly determines when and how a privileged trajectory should guide an on-policy student. At each turn, SMRC-SD verifies whether the student's current execution state matches a supported state along the reference trajectory. Distillation is applied only at matched states, filtering out turns for which the reference lacks locally compatible guidance. For each matched state, SMRC-SD further constructs state-conditioned teacher context from the successful trajectory, grounding supervision in the state actually reached. Across ALFWorld and WebShop, SMRC-SD consistently outperforms unconditional successful full-path distillation. With Qwen3-1.7B, it improves task success from $0.746$ to $0.865$ on ALFWorld and from $0.574$ to $0.693$ on WebShop. Controlled routing and context ablations support both selecting locally supported turns and constructing state-compatible teacher context as contributors to these gains. Code is available at https://github.com/liujunzhuo/SMRC-SD.

</details>


### [105] [Small Foundation Models of Human Cognition and Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05224)
*Nick Oh,Fernand Gobet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在分布内场景下，模型规模影响微乎其微，0.6B-1B参数即可匹配70B基线；但在分布外泛化时，更大模型优势显著。模型依赖刺激与反馈内容而非历史选择，且对试验顺序敏感。小型认知微调模型可作为心理实验噪声上限估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型经人类行为数据微调后可作为通用认知代理，但其所需规模及模型是处理任务结构还是利用统计捷径仍是开放问题，作者旨在探究规模效应与模型信息利用机制。

Method: 在Psych-101数据集（160个实验的1070万次试验级选择）上训练四个架构家族、参数从135M至14B的14个模型；通过逐步遮蔽提示通道（任务指令、实验刺激、结果反馈、选择历史）及打乱试验顺序两种诊断手段分析模型信息使用。

Result: 分布内场景下规模影响极小，0.6B-1B参数即可达到70B基线水平；分布外泛化呈现更陡峭的规模梯度，大模型对新颖任务结构泛化更优。遮蔽刺激与反馈内容导致75.7%信息损失并使模型低于随机水平；打乱顺序揭示模型对独立试验具有不变性，但对依赖先前响应的试验顺序敏感。

Conclusion: 小型认知微调模型在心理实验中可作为噪声上限估计器，但其适用范围仍受训练中所见范式限制。模型真正依赖任务结构信息而非简单统计捷径。

Abstract: Large language models fine-tuned on human behavioural data have emerged as general-purpose cognitive proxies, but the scale this requires, and whether these models process task structure or exploit statistical shortcuts, remain open questions. We train fourteen models from 135M to 14B parameters across four architecture families on Psych-101, a dataset of 10.7 million trial-level choices from 160 experiments. In-distribution, scale barely matters. The models fall within a narrow band, as though against a ceiling, and 0.6B to 1B parameters suffice to match a 70B baseline on held-out participants. Out-of-distribution, that band opens into a markedly steeper scaling gradient, with larger models clearly advantaged in generalisation to novel task structure. To determine what information these models use, we run two diagnostics. We progressively strip four prompt channels -- task instructions, experimental stimuli, outcome feedback, and choice history -- across 27 experiments, and permute trial order. Masking the content of stimuli and feedback destroys 75.7% of learned information and pushes models below chance, demonstrating that choice history alone does not account for performance. Permutation reveals invariance on tasks with independent trials but sensitivity where trial order is determined by prior responses. Small cognitively fine-tuned models therefore show promise as noise ceiling estimators for psychological experiments, though their scope remains bounded by the paradigms seen in training.

</details>


### [106] [Project2Task: Graph-Guided Project-Level Planning for Autonomous Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05225)
*Huirui Xu,Runtao Xu,Shuo Ren,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Project2Task，一个图引导的项目级规划层，通过创新原子和有向谱系图将研究项目分解为有界任务，显著提升了任务质量和执行准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究代理系统将研究项目视为单一超大任务，产生模糊或重叠的任务集，或依赖手动协调任务边界和执行顺序，缺乏对项目长期议程的有效规划。研究项目实际上是需要通过多个具有明确目标、并行备选方案和依赖感知序列的有界任务来推进的长期议程。

Method: 提出Project2Task图引导项目级规划层：1）将候选贡献表示为创新原子并组织为有向谱系图；2）使用轻量级伯努利块模型目标函数在水平、垂直和混合组合分解之间选择；3）生成具有明确贡献所有权的有界任务，修复重叠和缺失执行字段；4）输出包含目标、输入、预期产物、评估要求、边界约束、依赖关系和执行顺序的依赖感知任务合约。

Result: 在十个项目简报产生约30个任务的基准测试中，Project2Task平均质量得分为7.15，显著高于Brief基线的4.58和Topic-only设置的5.31。与AutoResearchClaw集成后，下游任务平均准确率从0.536提升至0.759。

Conclusion: 显式的项目到任务规划对产生连贯、非冗余和可执行的研究任务组合具有重要价值，证明了项目级规划层的有效性。

Abstract: Research agents can increasingly search literature, propose hypotheses, generate code, run experiments, and draft manuscripts from a single topic. However, a research project is not merely a larger task: it is a long-horizon agenda that must be advanced through multiple bounded tasks with distinct but related objectives, parallel alternatives, and dependency-aware sequences. Existing single-task systems often treat the project as one oversized task, produce a flat set of vague or overlapping tasks, or leave task boundaries and execution order to manual coordination. We introduce Project2Task, a graph-guided project-level planning layer for autonomous research. Given a project brief, it represents candidate contributions as innovation atoms and organizes them in a directed lineage graph. A lightweight Bernoulli block-model objective selects among horizontal, vertical, and hybrid portfolio decompositions. Project2Task then generates bounded tasks with explicit contribution ownership, repairs overlaps and missing execution fields, and emits dependency-aware task contracts that specify objectives, inputs, expected artifacts, evaluation requirements, boundary constraints, dependencies, and execution order. The contracts are independent of any particular downstream research executor and support integration of task outputs into a coherent project-level result. On a benchmark of ten project briefs yielding roughly 30 tasks, manuscript-based portfolio evaluation gives Project2Task an average quality score of 7.15, compared with 4.58 for the Brief Baseline and 5.31 for the Topic-only Setting. Integrating its contracts with AutoResearchClaw increases average downstream task accuracy from 0.536 to 0.759. These results demonstrate the value of explicit project-to-task planning for producing coherent, non-redundant, and executable research-task portfolios.

</details>


### [107] [TriQua: Reconciling Granularity and Context in Factuality Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05228)
*Jin Liu,Steffen Thoma,Achim Rettinger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了TriQua框架，通过灵活建模事实单元（简单声明表示为三元组，复杂声明表示为超关系事实）来平衡原子性与上下文完整性的权衡，并提供细粒度的错误检测和可解释性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM事实性评估中"先分解后验证"范式存在根本性权衡问题：原子事实（一句话传达一个信息单元）往往遗漏关键上下文，而更宽泛的陈述则缺乏精确评估所需的粒度。这种上下文保留与原子性之间的冲突需要一种自适应的建模方法。

Method: 提出TriQua框架：1) 根据复杂性自适应建模事实单元——简单声明提取为标准三元组，复杂声明通过附加辅助上下文限定符表示为超关系事实；2) 验证过程直接在特定三元组和限定符内注释具体错误，提供细粒度可解释性；3) 提出TriQuaScore量化结构化事实单元的事实性。

Result: 实验评估表明：1) TriQuaScore与人工标注的事实性分数高度一致；2) TriQua实现了稳健的分解质量；3) 在基于证据的事实验证中优于现有基于分解的框架。

Conclusion: TriQua通过自适应结构建模有效解决了原子事实分解中的上下文缺失问题，在保持原子性的同时保留了必要的上下文信息，为LLM事实性评估提供了更精确和可解释的方法。

Abstract: The "decompose-then-verify" paradigm for LLM factuality evaluation faces a fundamental trade-off: atomic facts, i.e., one sentence conveying one unit of information, often omit essential context, while broader statements lack the granularity needed for precise assessment. To address this, we introduce TriQua, a framework that flexibly models facts based on their complexity. Simple claims are extracted as standard triples, while complex claims are represented as hyperrelational facts by attaching auxiliary contextual qualifiers. This adaptive structure preserves the necessary context for accurate retrieval and verification without sacrificing atomicity. Furthermore, TriQua's verification process directly annotates concrete errors within specific triples and qualifiers, providing fine-grained explainability for error detection. Alongside the framework, we propose TriQuaScore to quantify the factuality of these structured fact units. Empirical evaluations show that TriQuaScore strongly aligns with human annotated factuality scores, TriQua achieves robust decomposition quality, and outperforms existing decomposition-based frameworks in evidence-based fact verification.

</details>


### [108] [Coherence-Oriented Dream Scene Visualisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05233)
*Azra Açıl,Simon Colton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究开发了Dream Scene Visualiser (DSV)系统，能将文字梦境描述转化为四帧连贯的图像序列，通过客观指标评估显示其在质量、忠实度和连贯性方面表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梦境通常具有强烈的情感体验但难以向他人传达，这种沟通障碍促使研究者开发一个能够可视化梦境描述的系统，帮助人们更好地分享和理解梦境内容。

Method: DSV系统采用两阶段方法：首先使用大型语言模型将梦境描述按时序分割为四个部分，然后利用文本到图像模型为每部分生成图像，通过特定机制保持图像序列间的视觉连贯性，并对与文本匹配不佳的图像进行重新生成。研究在DreamBank数据集上对50个可视化结果进行了评估。

Result: 通过CLIP、DINOv2和Qwen2-VL视觉语言模型的客观指标测量，系统在质量、忠实度和连贯性三个维度上均取得了量化评估结果，验证了DSV系统的有效性。

Conclusion: DSV系统成功实现了从文本梦境描述到时序图像序列的自动转化，为梦境的可视化分享提供了可行的技术方案，客观评估结果证明了系统的实用价值。

Abstract: Dreams can be emotionally intense but difficult to communicate. We describe the Dream Scene Visualiser (DSV) system which turns written dream descriptions into a temporal sequence of four panel images visualising the dream. This starts with a large language model prompted to split a dream description into four chronological parts. Then a text-to-image model produces images for each part with visual coherence maintained across the sequence, and DSV regenerates any image not suitably matching the text. We evaluate DSV over 50 visualisations from dream descriptions in DreamBank, and report quality, fidelity and coherence results via objective measures employing the CLIP, DINOv2 and Qwen2-VL vision-language models.

</details>


### [109] [Search2Skill: Skill Distillation Beyond Knowledge Boundaries Via Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05245)
*Muyang Ye,Tian Lan,Feihu Jiang,Yongshi Ye,Wuyunsiqin,Bin Zhu,Qianghuai Jia,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo,Ye Wang,Jinyang Zhang,Longyue Wang,Lingfeng Bao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Search2Skill框架，通过自动识别智能体能力缺口、搜索外部资源并提炼为可复用技能，突破了现有方法仅依赖模型内部知识的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自我演化技能学习方法仅从模型参数知识或轨迹中构建技能，受限于模型已知范围，而专业领域的规范和标准流程往往超出此边界，难以仅通过智能体自身获取。

Method: 提出Search2Skill框架，自动识别智能体能力缺口、搜索外部源解决缺口，并将检索证据提炼为结构化可复用技能；采用基于评分标准的强化学习方案，联合优化搜索时机、搜索方式和技能生成。

Result: 在三个基准的八个专家级领域中，Search2Skill在流式和留出评估协议下均持续优于搜索增强和基于轨迹的技能学习基线；进一步分析表明增益源于技能抽象而非原始检索证据，且获得的技能可跨模型规模迁移。

Conclusion: Search2Skill通过外部知识检索与技能抽象，有效突破了模型内部知识边界，实现了跨规模的可复用技能学习，为专家领域LLM智能体的自我演化提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Reusable skills, which encapsulate the procedural knowledge required to solve real-world professional tasks, offer LLM-based agents a path toward self-evolution in expert domains. Existing self-evolving skill methods construct skills internally from the model's parametric knowledge or trajectories, and are therefore bounded by what the model already knows. However, the domain conventions and standard procedures underlying professional skills often lie beyond this boundary and are hard to elicit from the agent alone. To address this issue, we therefore propose a novel framework, Search2Skill, that automatically identifies the agent's capability gaps, searches external sources to address them, and distills the retrieved evidence into structured, reusable skills. Specifically, Search2Skill is optimized by a rubric-based reinforcement learning scheme that jointly improves when to search, how to search, and how to generate skills. Experiments on eight expert-level domains from three benchmarks show that Search2Skill consistently outperforms both search-augmented and trajectory-based skill-learning baselines under both streaming and held-out evaluation protocols. Further analyses show that the gains arise from skill abstraction rather than raw retrieved evidence, and that the acquired skills transfer across model scales.

</details>


### [110] [LUNAR: Benchmarking Personalized Large Language Models on UNiversal User BehAvioR Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05246)
*Jiahao Zhang,Yongzhi Tong,Zelin Fu,Pengde Zhao,Yanmei Jiang,Jiang Feng,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了LUNAR基准，用于评估LLM基于跨领域日常生活行为历史进行个性化回复的能力。实验发现，行为日志访问是深度个性化的必要非充分条件，细粒度记录检索优于压缩记忆，但强个性化可能牺牲隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化LLM基准主要依赖文本角色或孤立行为信号，无法评估需要基于异构日常活动（衣食住行）进行跨领域行为个性化的场景，存在明显缺口。

Method: 提出LUNAR基准，这是首个基于纵向应用交互历史的跨领域日常生活个性化评估基准。采用多阶段粗到细合成流程构建数据，既支持可扩展基准构建，又缓解数据稀疏和隐私问题，且比其他合成基准更接近真实行为分布。

Result: 在19个主流LLM上的实验表明：(1)行为日志访问是深度个性化的必要非充分条件；(2)更多上下文或更大模型并不保证更好性能；(3)直接检索细粒度行为记录一致优于压缩记忆；(4)更强的个性化可能以隐私保护为代价。有效个性化依赖于跨领域证据的选择与整合。

Conclusion: 研究识别出证据选择、跨领域整合和隐私控制是个性化LLM的关键挑战，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Existing personalized LLM benchmarks primarily rely on textual personas or isolated behavioral signals, providing limited evaluation of cross-domain behavioral personalization, where responses must be grounded in heterogeneous daily-life activities. To address this gap, we introduce LUNAR, the first benchmark for evaluating how LLMs personalize responses from longitudinal app interaction histories across universal daily-life domains, including clothing, food, housing, and mobility. To support scalable benchmark construction while mitigating data sparsity and privacy concerns, LUNAR uses a multi-stage coarse-to-fine synthesis pipeline grounded in real-world behavioral patterns. Fidelity analyses show closer alignment with real behavioral distributions than other synthetic benchmarks. Experiments on 19 mainstream LLMs show that access to behavioral logs is necessary but not sufficient for deep personalization: neither more context nor larger models guarantees better performance; effective personalization depends on selecting and integrating relevant evidence across domains. Direct retrieval of fine-grained behavioral records consistently outperforms compressed memory, while stronger personalization can come at the cost of privacy protection. These findings identify evidence selection, cross-domain integration, and privacy control as key challenges for personalized LLMs.

</details>


### [111] [WorldClaw: Agentic 3D Open-World Generation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05248)
*Chunchao Guo,Jinpeng Li,Yang Li,Zilong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了WorldClaw，一个从文本生成大规模可探索3D世界的智能框架，解决了全局空间一致性、局部内容丰富性和资产可编辑性三大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以同时满足大规模3D世界生成的三个核心需求：全局空间一致性、丰富的局部内容细节、以及支持下游编辑和复用的显式资产，因此需要一个新的框架来统一解决这些问题。

Method: 采用由粗到细的智能体框架：(1) 规划智能体将文本转换为结构化规格，定义区域、地形、资产、材质和空间关系；(2) 从语义布局构建全局一致的地形基础；(3) 为细节区域生成地形条件组合，重建可编辑网格并恢复其在地形上的位置；(4) 基于渲染的智能体迭代优化地形、物体、外观和接触细节。

Result: 在多样化的开放世界提示词测试中，WorldClaw成功生成了具有一致空间组织、视觉吸引力的局部内容以及可编辑实例级资产的大规模场景，同时保持了统一的全局地形结构。

Conclusion: WorldClaw证明了通过智能体协作和由粗到细的策略，可以实现开放世界3D场景的高质量生成，在全局一致性与局部细节之间取得平衡，并支持资产的编辑与复用。

Abstract: Generating large-scale, freely explorable 3D worlds from open-ended text remains challenging because a system must jointly maintain global spatial coherence, rich local content, and explicit assets suitable for downstream editing and reuse. We present WorldClaw, a fully agentic, coarse-to-fine framework for open-world 3D scene generation. Planning agents translate a text prompt into a structured specification of regions, terrain, assets, materials, and spatial relations. WorldClaw then builds a globally coherent terrain foundation from semantic layouts, reusable assets, generative or procedural materials, and a region-aware height field. For detail-demanding regions, it generates terrain-conditioned compositions, reconstructs editable textured meshes, and recovers their placement on the terrain; render-based agents further refine terrain, objects, appearance, and contacts. Across diverse open-world prompts, WorldClaw produces large-scale scenes with coherent spatial organization, visually compelling local content, and editable instance-level assets while preserving a consistent global terrain structure.

</details>


### [112] [Posture and Sustainment Optimization Under Adversarial Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05256)
*Amelie Norris,Alyssa Lee,Natan Vidra,Spurthi Setty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对军事资产预先部署问题的鲁棒优化方法（CEV和RobustCEV），通过对抗性建模显著优于传统贪婪算法，在印太环境实验中效率提升高达158%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合行动规划中的预先部署（pre-commitment posture）问题至今未得到形式化解决。现有实践依赖贪婪启发式方法，仅最大化价值而忽略地理覆盖，在面对针对高战略价值位置攻击的对手时存在结构性脆弱性。

Method: 将预先部署与可持续性分配（PSA）问题建模为资产、战区位置和时间步上的有限视野马尔可夫决策过程。提出复合期望值（CEV）优化器，通过最大化威胁场景分布上的场景加权期望部署效率来配置资产；并扩展为RobustCEV方法，通过迭代应对根据观察到的部署更新目标分布的贝叶斯对手。

Result: 在印太基地环境（20项资产、5个战区位置）的三组实验中表明：（1）贪婪基线因地理覆盖不足承受25.1%的永久部署效率惩罚，在价值相关对抗威胁下出现57.3%的场景加权战备崩溃；（2）当威胁分布携带地理信号时，CEV优化器相比贪婪方法恢复高达19.8%的效率，5至20个精选场景足以捕获大部分收益；（3）当自适应对手采用欺骗性威胁先验时，RobustCEV扩展相对朴素优化器恢复高达158%的效率。所有发现通过Bonferroni校正的配对t检验和两级方差分解验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的场景加权对抗鲁棒优化引擎有效解决了预先部署问题中的地理覆盖不足和对抗脆弱性问题，为联合行动规划提供了形式化的解决方案，性能提升具有统计显著性且为策略的结构性属性而非采样伪影。

Abstract: Pre-commitment posture, the assignment of military assets to theater locations before conflict scenarios resolve, is a critical and formally unsolved problem in joint operational planning. Current practice relies on greedy heuristics that maximize value and ignore geographic coverage and are structurally vulnerable to adversaries that target high-strategic value locations. This paper presents a scenario-weighted adversarially robust posture optimization engine for the Posture and sustainability allocation (PSA) problem, modeled as a finite-horizon Markov Decision Process over assets, theater locations, and time steps. We introduce the Composite Expected Value (CEV) optimizer, which places assets by maximizing scenario-weighted expected posture efficiency over a distribution of threat scenarios, and the RobustCEV extension, which iterates against a Bayesian adversary that updates its targeting distribution in response to observed placement. Across three experiments in an Indo-Pacific basing environment with 20 assets and 5 theater locations, we demonstrate that: (1) the greedy baseline incurs a permanent 25.1% posture efficiency penalty due to geographic under-coverage and a 57.3% scenario-weighted readiness collapse under value-correlated adversarial threat; (2) the CEV optimizer recovers up to 19.8% efficiency over greedy when the threat distribution carries a geographic signal, with a curated set of 5 to 20 scenarios sufficient to capture the majority of this gain; and (3) the RobustCEV extension recovers up to 158% efficiency relative to a naive optimizer when an adaptive adversary employs a deceptive threat prior. All findings are validated using paired t-tests with Bonferroni correction and two-level variance decomposition, confirming that the performance gaps reported are structural properties of placement strategies rather than sampling artifacts.

</details>


### [113] [OrchestraBench: Evaluating Multi-Agent Orchestration Failure Modes, Recovery, and Decomposition Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05263)
*Yidian Chen,Yingzi Gu,Natan Vidra,Spurthi Setty,Sharon Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OrchestraBench是一个针对多智能体编排框架的基准测试，通过可控的故障注入机制来评估故障、恢复和级联传播，揭示了不同路由策略的鲁棒性差异和三层故障恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体编排框架基准测试主要关注任务准确率，缺乏对失败原因、级联起点和路由决策错误的诊断能力。随着这些框架从演示转向生产环境，迫切需要能够系统评估故障处理和恢复能力的基准。

Method: 提出OrchestraBench基准，采用可控的、种子可复现的故障注入机制，在企业工作流模板上进行测试。引入级联半径和单故障模式恢复率作为核心指标，使用Bootstrap置信区间和配对检验比较路由策略，并通过Claude智能体在可验证的算术依赖链上进行受控机制探测。

Result: 在26个金标准诊断案例中，关键词/标志路由器在对抗性案例上得分为0%，而意图推理模型路由器得分100%，匹配 oracle 表现。发现五类MAST故障模式呈现三层恢复能力：工具故障完全恢复(1.0)，模糊委托部分恢复(0.30)，三类潜在/语义故障永不恢复(0.0)。级联半径随管道深度增加（从0.9增至4.7），盲目重试会重现潜在故障并延长检测时间。

Conclusion: 检测和归因对于故障遏制至关重要，表面信号不可靠时意图推理路由显著优于关键词路由，可信状态信号是遏制收益的主要来源而非自主检测，这些受控链机制探测结果为多智能体系统的故障诊断和恢复策略设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Multi-agent orchestration frameworks are moving from demos to production, yet benchmarks typically report task accuracy without diagnosing why a pipeline failed, where a cascade began, or which routing decision caused the breakdown. OrchestraBench evaluates failure, recovery, and decomposition through a controlled, seed-reproducible failure-injection harness over templated enterprise workflows. It introduces cascade radius and per-failure-mode recovery as primary metrics and compares routing policies with bootstrap confidence intervals and paired tests. On a 26-case gold-labelled diagnostic, a keyword/flag router scored 0% on adversarial cases with misleading or missing surface flags, whereas an intent-reasoning model router scored 100%, matching the oracle. Controlled mechanism probes with a real Claude agent over a verifiable arithmetic dependency chain revealed three failure-handling tiers across five MAST modes: tool faults recovered fully (1.0), ambiguous delegation recovered partially (0.30), and three latent or semantic modes never recovered (0.0). This ordering persisted when the computation was reframed as a loan-approval workflow and across Sonnet, Opus, and Haiku, although absolute rates shifted with context. Blind retry reproduced latent faults and increased time to detection, indicating that detection and attribution are necessary for containment. Cascade radius increased with pipeline depth (mean 0.9 to 4.7 across depths 3-7). A trusted-state repair ablation showed that apparent containment gains primarily came from the trusted-state signal rather than autonomous detection. These results are controlled-chain mechanism probes, not domain-workload claims.

</details>


### [114] [Agentic self-driving microscopy benchmarks support qualification but do not necessarily generalize to unseen tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05266)
*Nathan S Johnson,Ian Abshire*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了基准测试和日志框架，系统评估了不同大语言模型智能体架构在显微镜控制任务中的表现，发现基准测试适合已知任务的验证和比较，但无法可靠预测智能体在未知新任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学表征工具（如显微镜、同步辐射束线）的大语言模型智能体控制研究刚刚起步，缺乏成熟的工程范式。在设计智能体系统时面临诸多选择（如LLM选择、智能体数量、职责划分、RAG参数等），研究者不仅需要确保智能体能正确执行已知任务，还希望其能泛化到未曾见过的新任务，因此需要系统性评估框架。

Method: 开发了基准测试和追踪日志框架，系统评估了105种智能体配置在53个显微镜基准测试上的表现。评估维度包括：一/二/三智能体图拓扑结构、五种LLM、RAG与上下文参数、操作约束等。共执行1,949次测试运行，记录了49,109次RAG检索，并使用代理模型尝试预测智能体在新任务上的表现。

Result: 直接比较显示不同配置在延迟、令牌使用、成本和失败模式上存在明显差异。然而，基于智能体架构和测试结果训练的代理模型无法可靠预测智能体在新未知任务上的表现。当前异构测试套件不支持构建任务无关的全局配置模型。

Conclusion: 这些基准测试在资格认证、回归测试、诊断分析和直接比较方面具有实用价值，但仅依靠基准测试无法预测智能体在未知任务上的泛化能力。研究为显微镜智能体控制系统的设计提供了系统性评估方法，同时揭示了当前基准测试框架的局限性。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly being developed to control a wide range of scientific characterization tools including microscopes and synchrotron beamlines. Research into agentic control of physical infrastructure is nascent and there are few well-established paradigms for how to engineer an agentic system. There are many choices to make when designing a microscopy agent, including the choice of LLM, the number of agents to use, agent responsibilities and delegation rules, retrieval-augmented generation parameters, and more. When designing and optimizing an agentic microscope controller, researchers not only want to ensure that the agent can correctly perform known tasks but also that the agent can generalize to new tasks that it has not encountered before. In this study, we develop a benchmark and trace-logging framework that reveals a) how different choices of agent architecture impact performance at microscopy tasks and b) the limitations of benchmarks for predicting if a particular agent will perform well on unseen microscopy tasks. The framework was used to evaluate one-, two-, and three-agent graph topologies, five LLMs, RAG and context parameters, and operational constraints across 53 microscopy benchmark tests. In total, 105 agent configurations, 1,949 individual test runs, and 49,109 RAG retrievals were recorded. Direct comparisons showed clear differences in latency, token use, cost, and failure mode between configurations. However, surrogate models trained on agent architecture and test results did not reliably predict an agent's performance on new, unseen tasks. These results show that these benchmarks are useful for qualification, regression testing, diagnosis, and direct comparison, but the current heterogeneous test suite does not support a task-independent global configuration model.

</details>


### [115] [CASCADE: An Agentic Regulatory Network Framework for Patient-Data-Validated Downstream Perturbation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05359)
*Jose A. Bird*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CASCADE是一个基于ARACNe调控网络预测基因扰动下游转录效应的智能框架。研究通过基因拷贝数扩增作为剂量代理验证预测方向，发现MYC预测效果良好，但扩展到其他基因时效果不一。同时测试了LLM代理将自然语言请求转化为工具调用的能力，准确率达71.4%-85.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基因扰动预测工具的验证方法存在局限性，仅检查预测基因是否为已知癌症基因（成员关系），而未验证预测的变化方向是否符合现实。研究者希望开发一种更严格的验证方法，通过真实肿瘤数据检验预测方向的准确性，同时评估LLM代理在自然语言处理中的可靠性。

Method: 1) 使用ARACNe预计算调控网络构建CASCADE智能框架，通过MCP接口提供服务；2) 创新性地使用基因拷贝数扩增作为敲低反操作的剂量代理，利用TCGA真实肿瘤数据进行验证；3) 在MYC及15个其他基因上测试，并与MSigDB基因集基线进行比较；4) 单独测试LLM代理将自然语言请求转化为CASCADE工具调用的能力（35个查询）。

Result: MYC预测验证：在三种癌症类型中显示强一致性（BRCA 90.0%、COAD 72.0%、STAD 85.7%，p<0.0013），超过置换基线，在METABRIC独立队列中复现（87.2%）。但与MSigDB基线相比，未显示超越现有MYC/E2F生物学知识。扩展验证：15个基因中，增殖机制调控因子大多复现成功，但谱系身份转录因子和CCND2一致失败。LLM代理测试：本地模型达到71.4%精确匹配，较大模型达85.7%；模式和基因别名失败可通过规模或服务器端修正解决，但两个模型在模糊查询时都自信地默认为错误的扰动类型。

Conclusion: CASCADE框架在特定基因（如MYC）的转录效应预测方向上表现良好，但验证效果因基因而异，增殖机制调控因子效果较好而谱系转录因子效果不佳。LLM代理在工具调用方面达到合理准确率，但在处理模糊查询时存在系统性失败。研究揭示了当前方法的局限性和未来改进方向。

Abstract: CASCADE is an agentic framework that predicts downstream transcriptional effects of gene perturbation from precomputed
  ARACNe regulatory networks, exposed via MCP. Prior work validates such tools by checking whether predicted genes are
  known cancer genes (membership); we instead test whether the predicted direction of change matches reality, using
  focal-gene copy-number amplification as a dosage-based proxy for the inverse of knockdown against real TCGA patient
  tumor data.
  For MYC, CASCADE's predicted knockdown targets show strong concordance with real amplified-vs-non-amplified tumor
  expression across three cancer types (BRCA: 90.0%, COAD: 72.0%, STAD: 85.7%; all p<0.0013), well above permutation
  baselines, surviving a PAM50 subtype control and replicating in an independent cohort (METABRIC, 87.2%). Compared
  against curated MSigDB gene-set baselines via Fisher's exact test, CASCADE's accuracy is not shown to exceed existing
  public knowledge of MYC- or E2F-driven biology, though its gene-specific direction-calling clearly outperforms a naive
  uniform guess.
  Extending to fifteen additional genes, validation proves gene-specific rather than universal: proliferation-machinery
  regulators mostly replicate, while lineage-identity transcription factors and one cyclin-D paralog (CCND2)
  consistently fail, a pattern we discuss as a hedged, post-hoc hypothesis.
  We separately benchmark whether an LLM-based agent correctly grounds natural-language requests into CASCADE's real MCP
  tool calls. Across 35 queries, a documented local model reaches 71.4% exact match (85.7% for a larger model); schema
  and gene-alias failures are resolved by scale or server-side correction, but both models confidently default to the
  wrong perturbation type on ambiguous queries, a failure a targeted fix could not resolve because its trigger condition
  never occurs.

</details>


### [116] [Counterfactual Analysis via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05367)
*Zonghao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在在线借贷场景中利用GPT-3.5进行反事实分析，结果显示通过提示工程，GPT的预测R²从1.97%提升至2.84%，接近梯度提升回归的3.48%，展现了LLM在反事实分析中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实分析对于决策制定具有重要价值，能够预测假设情景下的潜在结果。在在线借贷领域，反事实投资回报率（ROI）对于评估不同利率方案至关重要。研究旨在探索大语言模型（LLM）在此类分析中的应用潜力。

Method: 采用GPT-3.5模型进行反事实分析研究。首先评估GPT的预测性能并与先进机器学习算法进行比较；通过提示工程优化GPT的预测能力；随后利用GPT生成不同替代利率下的反事实ROI。

Result: 提示工程显著提升了GPT的预测性能，R²从1.97%提高至2.84%，接近梯度提升回归算法的3.48%水平。GPT在生成反事实ROI时表现出逻辑连贯性和因果推理能力。

Conclusion: 研究证明了大语言模型作为反事实分析工具有效性，特别是在在线借贷领域的应用价值，并暗示LLM在各种预测和决策场景中具有更广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Counterfactual analysis aims to predict potential outcomes under hypothetical scenarios, offering valuable insights for decision-making. This paper investigates the application of large language models (LLMs), specifically the GPT-3.5 model, for counterfactual analysis. We focus on the online lending context, where the counterfactual return on investment (ROI) is crucial for evaluating different interest rate schemes. We begin by assessing the predictive performance of GPT and comparing it with advanced machine learning algorithms. The results show that prompt engineering can significantly enhance GPT's predictions, with the R-squared increasing from 1.97% to 2.84%, closely approaching the 3.48% achieved by gradient-boosted regression. Subsequently, we utilize GPT to generate counterfactual ROIs under a set of alternative interest rates. GPT exhibits logical coherence and causal reasoning in its responses. The findings underscore the potential of LLMs as effective tools for counterfactual analysis in online lending, suggesting broader applications for LLMs in various predictive and decision-making contexts.

</details>


### [117] [C$^3$PO: Evaluating Cross-Modal Composition and Counterfactual Performance in Omnimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05381)
*Swapnanil Mukherjee,Agyeya Negi,Tanuja Ganu,Ponnurangam Kumaraguru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了C³PO基准测试，包含3404个跨模态样本，用于评估多模态大语言模型的信息组合和反事实冲突处理能力。研究发现最佳模型准确率仅73.17%，远低于人类的88.64%，86-95%的失败源于模态主导问题，模型过度依赖文本而忽略其他模态的矛盾证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型虽然能够处理多种感官输入，但其推理严重偏向主导模态（尤其是文本），导致跨模态推理脆弱。缺乏系统性基准来诊断模型在何时何地以及为何在跨模态推理中失败。

Method: 提出C³PO基准测试，包含3404个样本，覆盖视频、音频、图像和文本四种模态；设计两个评估任务：信息组合（融合分散证据）和反事实冲突（解决故意矛盾）；采用配对的IC/CC结构和四层设计；通过全自动流程构建，使用25个逻辑模板；使用注意力探针分析模型行为。

Result: 人类准确率88.64%，最佳模型Gemini-3.1-Pro仅73.17%；开源模型在冲突处理上崩溃；86-95%的失败源于模态主导，模型87-95%的注意力集中在文本上；中间层注意力熵可预测正确性；结构复杂度相同的模板间存在56分准确率差距，说明性能取决于模态在冲突解决中的结构角色。

Conclusion: 多模态感知并不保证鲁棒的推理；需要架构设计使模型能够维持跨模态注意力，避免过早收敛；当前模型存在严重的模态主导问题，需要针对性的架构改进来提升跨模态推理能力。

Abstract: Current Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can process diverse sensory inputs, yet their reasoning remains heavily biased toward a dominant modality, resulting in brittle cross-modal reasoning. We introduce C$^3$PO, a benchmark of 3,404 samples spanning video, audio, image, and text, evaluating two abilities: information composition (fusing dispersed evidence) and counterfactual conflict (resolving deliberate contradictions).
